The captured page exposes no comment thread or pagination controls; live browser expansion could not be verified. [Saved source](../data/glasp_261019/science.txt) · [Full inventory](../data/glasp_261019/inventory.json). Titles come only from the supplied source pages; publisher, library and critical sources establish identity, purpose and assessment evidence. Publisher promotion is not independent proof of quality.

## Method and reading priority

Fiction and primarily instructional or specialist technical works are removed before vetoes. All remaining non-vetoed works are scored, including poor fits. E, S and D use their own quadratic profiles; the other seven criteria remain linear. T11 and T12 are separate thematic deductions. H13 uses the assessed historical endpoint, P14 the original production year, and O15 one verified Amazon rating aggregate. Unknown Amazon counts receive the required zero-deduction fallback, not a claim of popularity. Calculations and sorting retain full precision; displayed values use two decimals.

Verdicts: Recommend ≥85; Consider ≥75; Selective ≥65; Low priority ≥50; Poor fit <50. Confidence describes evidence strength, not statistical probability. Reader aggregates are separate from the quality score. A missing rating is shown as unavailable; no rating is invented for Book Marks, LibraryThing, StoryGraph, Goodreads or Amazon.

The highest-ranked eligible works are *Energy And Civilization* (88.39), *Stuff Matters* (84.38), *Energy Myths and Realities* (83.94), *Midnight In Chernobyl* (83.88), *The Quest* (81.60). The lowest-ranked eligible works are *The Cancer Code* (31.18), *My Stroke of Insight* (29.10), *The Origin of Consciousness in the Breakdown of the Bicameral Mind* (21.56); low scores do not remove them from the recommendation ledger.

## Hard exclusions

No scores are assigned to these works. One underlying work can have several source-edition entries.

| Work | Rule · confidence | Reason and evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Anthropic Bias — Nick Bostrom | 2 · moderate | A specialist research monograph on observation selection, developing and defending formal anthropic principles for philosophy-of-science and cosmology specialists. It is not the broader general-reader argument of Superintelligence. [1](https://www.routledge.com/Anthropic-Bias-Observation-Selection-Effects-in-Science-and-Philosophy/Bostrom/p/book/9780415883948) Source entries: [Anthropic Bias Nick Bostrom](https://glasp.co/books/p/anthropic-bias-observation-selection-effects-in-science-and-philosophy). |
| End of Illness — David B. Agus | 2 · moderate | A practical personal-health program: recommendations about testing, prevention, diet and daily habits form the principal purpose. Its narrative explanations do not remove the instruction gate. [1](https://openlibrary.org/works/OL17343563W) [2](https://glasp.co/books/p/end-of-illness) Source entries: [End of Illness David B. Agus](https://glasp.co/books/p/end-of-illness). |
| Fundamentals of Plasma Physics — Paul M. Bellan | 2 · high | Plasma-physics textbook with mathematical development and problems for specialist study. [1](https://openlibrary.org/works/OL8323707W) [2](https://glasp.co/books/p/fundamentals-of-plasma-physics) Source entries: [Fundamentals of Plasma Physics Paul M. Bellan](https://glasp.co/books/p/fundamentals-of-plasma-physics). |
| History and Geography of Human Genes — L. Luca Cavalli-Sforza; Paolo Menozzi; Alberto Piazza | 2 · high | A specialist population-genetics reference and research synthesis, with extensive regional genetic-frequency data, methods and maps; its primary structure serves specialist inquiry rather than a general historical narrative. [1](https://www.jstor.org/stable/j.ctv301gjp) [2](https://www.degruyterbrill.com/document/doi/10.1515/9780691187266-toc/html) Source entries: [History and Geography of Human Genes L. Luca Cavalli-Sforza](https://glasp.co/books/p/history-and-geography-of-human-genes). |
| Learn or Die — Edward Hess | 2 · moderate | A program for building individual and organizational learning capabilities. Its structure applies a specialist management method, with cases supporting the instructional framework. [1](https://openlibrary.org/works/OL20004893W) [2](https://glasp.co/books/p/learn-or-die-using-science-to-build-a-leading-edge-learning-organization) Source entries: [Learn or Die Edward Hess](https://glasp.co/books/p/learn-or-die-using-science-to-build-a-leading-edge-learning-organization). |

## Full assessments

<a id="g0587"></a>
### Energy And Civilization

**Vaclav Smil; original production 1994. 88.39/100; Recommend. Score confidence: moderate.**

Retain the latest tactical reassessment, including D=9 and all other attainments. The broad energy-production history is explanatory, not an engineering handbook. Retain 1994 original production: MIT Press explicitly identifies Energy and Civilization as an updated and expanded version of Energy in World History, so the 2017 title does not reset age. Historical agency remains less fully explained than physical constraints.

The ten latest notes remain supported: E/M retain historical-causal reservations, S/U/F are unusually strong, D reflects information rather than difficulty, R/A/N/P retain durable authority with demanding structure.

- **E:** Retain 18. Quantitative source synthesis is strong, and explicit limits on energy-based explanation matter. Historical reconstruction and contested framing still prevent a near-perfect empirical-integrity claim. The new reviews do not audit all estimates or resolve the adverse historian's causal reservations.
- **S:** Retain 17. Physical energy, conversion technology, agricultural production, transport, industrial scale and economic development are connected throughout the account. The rubric requires integration of several relevant layers, not exhaustive coverage of every social-history subfield. Limited human agency is still a constraint on the scope of the causal explanation.
- **U:** Retain 14. Orders of magnitude, power density, conversion losses and infrastructure inertia transfer broadly. These constraints are more directly reusable than the book's ability to explain which political choices prevail.
- **M:** Retain 11.294117647058824. Strong mechanisms of energy conversion, productive limits and technology diffusion. Do not raise this to 12 while specialist criticism of historical context and causal selection remains unresolved.
- **D:** Raise 7 to 9. The earlier reason was density and reading difficulty, which misapplies this criterion. New reviews, including the adverse review, support a large amount of useful information in a coherent account. Boxes reduce disruption. Keep a one-point loss because the wide catalog of technical comparisons does not always yield an equally strong integrated historical explanation; there is no complete chapter-by-chapter audit establishing perfect economy.
- **R:** Retain 9. Long-run physical and infrastructure constraints provide durable value even as current energy shares change.
- **F:** Retain 7. Energy, engineering, industry, production and the material basis of civilization are central.
- **A:** Retain 5. Established specialist synthesis with sustained serious engagement, including critical attention rather than only publicity blurbs.
- **N:** Retain 2. Clear overall chronology and helpful boxes coexist with factual overload and a demanding reading experience. This is the appropriate place to retain the prior reading-access reservation.
- **P:** Retain 2. The conceptual and quantitative language is disciplined. Dryness is not imprecision.

<a id="g0639"></a>
### Stuff Matters

**Mark Miodownik; original production 2013. 84.38/100; Consider. Score confidence: moderate.**

Retain all ten attainments and no-veto decision. General materials explanation is the main purpose, with no procedural training structure. Technical reliability remains stronger than strategic breadth for this reader.

All ten prior notes remain supported: E/M/A are strong, S/U/F below the best industrial syntheses; D/R/N/P remain high for compact, durable explanation.

- **E:** Expert explanation of well-established material properties; simplified presentation prevents full attainment.
- **S:** Strong materials-to-application links, but less integration of trade, institutions and production systems.
- **U:** Useful design constraints and material intuition, with fewer strategic models.
- **M:** Explains how structure and properties enable applications at an accessible level.
- **D:** A compact selection of materials with little unnecessary technical apparatus.
- **R:** The basic material mechanisms are durable.
- **F:** Materials and engineering fit, but elementary physics adds less for this reader.
- **A:** Materials-science expertise directly supports the book's core.
- **N:** Everyday objects anchor clear and memorable explanations.
- **P:** Controlled explanations connect concrete experience to physical properties.

**Daniel Yergin; original production 2011. 81.60/100; Consider. Score confidence: moderate.**

Retain all ten attainments and no-veto finding. Energy provision, investment and infrastructure are central, with politics and climate as interacting domains. The historical and prospective mixture has no single central historical endpoint.

The ten prior notes remain supported: E/A reflect access with independence limits; S/U/M/F retain infrastructure integration; D/R/N/P retain narrative length and dated outlook reservations.

- **U:** Useful for energy-system dependencies and investment constraints.
- **M:** Good system explanation; some episodes remain event and personality driven.
- **D:** Large volume and extended episodes impose a substantial reading cost.
- **R:** Structural history lasts; market and technology outlooks are dated.
- **F:** Energy systems and industrial infrastructure fit directly.
- **A:** Long-standing specialist authority and industry access.
- **N:** Clear large-scale narrative despite its length.
- **P:** Generally controlled explanatory prose.

<a id="g0604"></a>
### Ignition!

**John Drury Clark; original production 1972. 81.40/100; Consider. Score confidence: moderate.**

Retain all ten attainments and no-veto finding. Narrative participant history dominates chemistry detail; it does not train readers to formulate propellants. The assessed endpoint remains about 1970.

E/A retain firsthand authority with selection limits. S/U/M/F retain concrete design constraints. D/R/N/P retain dense, durable, memorable history and occasional humorous overstatement.

- **E:** Firsthand scientific history, with memoir selection and perspective limits.
- **S:** Chemistry, testing, handling, procurement and performance interact; wider economy is less developed.
- **U:** Excellent lessons about optimisation under competing practical constraints.
- **M:** Failure modes and trade-offs have unusually concrete physical explanations.
- **D:** Compact technical history with substantial information per episode.
- **R:** Experimental discipline and physical trade-offs remain durable.
- **F:** Engineering, high-tech development and reliability fit directly.
- **A:** Author was a direct participant in propellant research.
- **N:** Memorable narrative makes difficult material manageable.
- **P:** Humour is effective but occasionally displaces exact qualification.

<a id="g0534"></a>
### Scale

**Geoffrey West; original production 2017. 76.36/100; Consider. Score confidence: moderate.**

Retain all ten attainments and the no-veto decision after reading the earlier Dyson/Fix-based assessment. Biological scaling has stronger support than universal claims about cities and firms. The present catalog adds no evidence of quality. General explanation passes rule 2; neither urban applications nor speculative forecasts dominate the entire work.

E/S/U/M retain limits on universality and causal derivation. D/N remain constrained by digressions. R/F/A/P retain strong scientific fit and authority with overstatement risk.

- **E:** Empirical regularities deserve attention; universality and causal extrapolation into social systems remain disputed.
- **S:** Connects networks, biological constraints, cities and companies, with weaker integration of institutions and changing incentives.
- **U:** Useful dimensional and scaling heuristics, but limited support for direct business or policy prediction.
- **M:** Scaling correlations are not always accompanied by a persuasive causal derivation.
- **D:** Lengthy digressions and repeated claims reduce density for a mathematically trained reader.
- **R:** Scaling questions endure; some proposed universal social laws may not.
- **F:** Scaling is central, but much space goes to familiar popular physics, biology and cities.
- **A:** The author is a leading contributor to the research being explained; authority does not settle its generality.
- **N:** Accessible diagrams and stories coexist with a long, digressive structure.
- **P:** Clear qualitative language sometimes implies more universality than the evidence supports.

<a id="g0669"></a>
### The Rise of Superman
